### MC-05 — Enable / disable per machine ('files stay on disk') *(HARD; recommended: design)*

- **Condition broken:** The bar's 'NOT SIMPLE: ... a mechanism that does not exist (per-machine disable while files stay on disk and Claude Code still loads .claude/skills)'. Forcing lines: .planning/specs/2026-09-02-phase-1-build.md:138 @ b5c0507 (the Placer copies straight into the agent's skills directory - placement IS loading) and src/lib/schema.ts:100 @ b5c0507 (a placements entry has no enabled flag, and adding one changes nothing on disk).
- **CLI side:** HARD — The drawn promise is 'files stay on disk AND Claude Code stops loading them'. Claude Code loads whatever sits in .claude/skills, and the CLI's architecture makes placement IDENTICAL to loading (spec:138). So a disable must physically move the folder out of the skills root, which means: a new machine-local holding directory (not in section 4.2's enumerated tree, spec:105-114, and therefore not swept by bare uninstall, src/commands/uninstallMachine.ts:120 @ b5c0507 - leaving orphaned skill folders behind after 'remove terum-skills from this machine'); a Placer move-and-restore path beside the existing quarantine one, against invariant 33's 'nothing is ever written inside a placed folder' discipline and the repo's one-path rule (spec:545); a new sync classification so the reconciler does not re-place a disabled skill (src/commands/sync.ts:164-172 @ b5c0507 re-places from the people file eve
- **Meanwhile, app side:** FORBIDDEN (D20 no) — An app-owned `disabled: true` per skill would grey the card and flip the switch while the folder stayed in ~/.claude/skills and Claude Code went on loading it every session. Refused.

### PF-08 — Opt out of publishing install records (Settings S275) *(HARD; recommended: design)*

- **Condition broken:** Three at once, plus a missing mechanism. (i) "a privacy call" - the bar lists it NOT SIMPLE unqualified. (ii) a change to what a DECIDED signal MEANS team-wide: D38 (.planning/specs/2026-09-01-team-skill-sharing.md:140) says in terms that "rated" IS the install count from committed people files, and its sole implementation `installCounts` (src/lib/readme.ts:40) has no notion of a suppressed record, so every consumer (src/lib/readme.ts:87, src/commands/ls.ts:60, the "12 teammates use this" facepile at build.py:988) would silently start reporting a floor as a count. (iii) "a mechanism that does not exist" - a placement that is legitimately unrecorded. Forcing lines: src/commands/sync.ts:170 (the update pass skips a placement absent from `installed`), src/commands/sync.ts:316 with :63 and :289 (hook-mode deferral makes the team `incomplete`, so it is never stamped and every session start does a full pull), src/commands/sync.ts:326-329 and :337-339 (orphan adoption republishes the record; refusal writes a permanent `declined`), src/commands/uninstall.ts:105 (an endorsed-skill uninstall on a suppressed machine writes a permanent id-wide decline). Note deliberately that the RECOMMENDED design disclosure does not close this gap - unlike PF-05, where the recommended relabel delivers the column's value honestly, here the disclosure documents the control's absence rather than substituting for it. That is why final_verdict tracks the control and not the recommendation.
- **CLI side:** HARD — The coster's load-bearing mechanism claim is false at b5c0507 and I verified every line. Its delta said "`sync` and `uninstall-skill` are unaffected because they key off the machine-local `placements` ledger, not the committed record"; the code says the opposite - sync reconciles the machine-local ledger AGAINST the committed `installed` array. (1) src/commands/sync.ts:170 SKIPS a placement whose id is not in `person.installed` and not in `pending`, so a suppressed install would never be refreshed to a new version again. (2) `reconcileOrphans` (src/commands/sync.ts:302-343) makes every suppressed placement an ORPHAN: interactively it prompts "Adopt orphaned placement at ...?" (:317) and on yes re-writes the record (:326-329), undoing the opt-out; on no it appends to `declined` (:337-339), which per phase-1-build.md:183/:193 suppresses ALL automatic placement of that ID forever. (3) In ho
- **Meanwhile, app side:** NA (D20 no) — nothing coherent. Either the app suppresses a CLI write (the app owning a CLI write path, which the seam forbids), or it hides the record from me alone while it stays committed and visible to everyone else - a privacy control that protects nobody.

### AC-04 — Resize, minimum window size, reflow, real scroll containers, portalled overlays *(MEDIUM; recommended: both)*

- **Condition broken:** The ALSO-SIMPLE clause 'an app rule' (singular). This is sixteen rules with sixteen measured binding widths (gap-8.md:214-231), plus a test artefact class the project does not have - the fidelity gate is pinned at 1440x900 (gap-8.md:196-197: 'Every rule in section 3 needs its own Playwright assertion at its own width, or the rules exist only in prose'), plus a SECOND, app-authored fidelity baseline for three boards that no design PNG can validate (gap-8.md:454-456), plus one open D20-adjacent fork (T-E, gap-8.md:485). It stays MEDIUM rather than HARD because it is bounded and additive - gap-8 enumerates every region - and no new subsystem or mechanism is introduced. The D-W1 decision alone (gap-8.md:141) IS simple and should be recorded immediately; the reflow table is the part that is not.
- **CLI side:** NA — Window geometry, reflow and stacking are frontend structure. Nothing CLI-side is implied; the only CLI fact that bears on it is that rendered payloads are unbounded (src/commands/ls.ts:139 @ b5c0507).
- **Meanwhile, app side:** MEDIUM (D20 no) — Two separable pieces. SIMPLE now: adopt D-W1 (min 1200x720, the work-area clamp, window-state persistence), make every list/section body a real scroll container with a sticky header, portal the overlays under one z-index scale, and narrow user-select: none to chrome. MEDIUM, and the reason the row is MEDIUM: the sixteen-region reflow table with a measured binding width per region (gap-8.md:214-231 - rail 1396, Library grid 1260, Marketplace 1320/1280, roster 1213, report measure 1306, dialogs, tooltips, the Settings nav/body), sixteen Playwright assertions at sixteen widths because the fidelity gate never leaves 1440x900 (gap-8.md:196-197), and a second app-authored baseline for SkillDetail / MarketplaceProject / MarketplacePerson that no design PNG can validate (gap-8.md:454-456). Tests: layout.test.ts, one per region, each at its own width; scroll.test.ts: a 200-row list scrolls and the header stays fixed; overlay.test.ts: a tooltip at the right edge wraps instead of overflowing; roster.test.ts: a dropped column's value is still reachable (blocked on T-E).
- **Cites:** `src/commands/ls.ts:139 @ b5c0507`, `src/commands/ls.ts:142 @ b5c0507`

### AC-18 — Accessibility beyond colour contrast is unaddressed *(MEDIUM; recommended: app)*

- **Condition broken:** Cardinality — bar condition (5) 'a one-paragraph spec delta to ONE section', and the app-side allowance 'an app rule' (singular). AC-18 is an accessibility SECTION of six-plus clauses. Forcing lines: build.py:1546-1553, scrim at build.py:1549 (position: absolute inside the 1440×900 frame — the port needs a portalled, focus-trapped overlay primitive that every drawn dialog inherits; gap-8.md:436 makes the same call citing the pre-snapshot :1548); frontend-stack.md:333 with gap-8.md:442 (the @axe-core/playwright gate over every route in both themes is proposed, not written, and is a test job, not a paragraph). Additionally forcing the narrowing of clause 3, and the reason an unqualified SIMPLE here would have hidden a CLI cost: src/lib/prompt.ts:14-20 @ b5c0507 (Prompter has no progress member; the bar names adding one as NOT SIMPLE), src/commands/sync.ts:32 / :71 / :278 / :294 @ b5c0507 (notice() fires on problems; 'Skills synchronized.' is the single completion line; SyncResult is a terminal aggregate returned once), corroborated by the design's own comment at build.py:4010.
- **CLI side:** NA — Roles, accessible names, focus order, live regions, reduced motion and text selection are properties of the rendered document. No repo file, verb, flag or guard participates, and nothing touches team.json, the §6.0 permitted-writes table, privacy or the D-R4 sync stamp. The only CLI-adjacent dependencies are what a live region can honestly ANNOUNCE: mid-flight per-phase progress has no producer (src/lib/prompt.ts:14-20, src/commands/sync.ts:32/:71/:278/:294 @ b5c0507) and belongs to RM-17, and the eval-run stream is out of scope entirely — no board draws it and D27 is PROPOSED, not decided.
- **Meanwhile, app side:** MEDIUM (D20 ok) — The desktop spec records an accessibility SECTION and the port implements it as it goes rather than retrofitting. SHIP NOW — five clauses, each individually a SIMPLE app rule, and none of them should be dropped because the row totals to MEDIUM: (1) Roles and names, at authoring time — nav rows → <a> inside <nav>; tabs → the tab pattern with aria-selected; the enable switch → <button role=\"switch\" aria-checked>; the role chip → a menu button with aria-haspopup=\"menu\"; icon-only buttons take aria-label from the tooltip text the design already wrote (tooltip(), build.py:463); plus <html lang=\"en\"> per gap-8 D-W3 §5.2 obligation 1, since grep -c 'lang=' → 0 fails WCAG 3.1.1 Level A. (2) A written focus order: top bar → sidebar → panel header → panel body → rail. (3) role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\" on the drawn first-sync progress card (progress_card(), build.py:4026-4027, used by onboarding_boot() :4037-4042), announcing PHASE TRANSITIONS ONLY — narrowed from the coster's 'each phase row announcing its completion', see the carve-outs. (4) One @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) block written WITH the first motion that is added, zeroing durations and explicitly stopping any skeleton shimmer and any progress-bar animation — there is nothing to reduce today (grep → 0), which is exactly why the rule must be written now rather than retrofitted. (5) user-select: none narrows from frame-wide to chrome (top bar, sidebar, tab strip) at BOTH sites, build.py:301 and build.py:3965, so content, printed commands and error strings become selectable. THE EXCESS that makes the row MEDIUM rather than SIMPLE, all additive and all app-side: (6) a portalled, focus-trapped overlay primitive — dialogs trap focus, restore to the invoker, close on Esc and on scrim click, and the scrim becomes fixed/portalled instead of position: absolute inside the frame (build.py:1549); every drawn dialog inherits it, so it is a shared overlay MECHANISM, not a rule. (7) an a11y test job — @axe-core/playwright@4.13.0 over every route in both themes (frontend-stack.md:333, listed there as a proposal in a stack that does not exist yet; gap-8.md:442 says the same and expects it to fail loudly on its first run). CARVE-OUTS, written as dependencies rather than promises: the mid-flight 'N of M' count the design draws at build.py:4035 has no producer and is RM-17's cost, not this row's; and the eval-run live region is OUT OF SCOPE — no board draws it (skill_detail_run_eval() is a confirm dialog, build.py:1604-1608) and D27 is PROPOSED, i.e. undecided, so it is not 'DEFERRED' either. Porting check_contrast()'s 80 pairs as a unit test is AC-13's own row and is cited, not absorbed.

The consequence text per row:

- **AC-02** — One decision, one board's worth: the panel-left glyph at build.py:247 needs either a collapsed-sidebar state (no board exists; an icon-rail sidebar is explicitly out of scope per gap-8.md:451) or removal from section_header. Both other collapsed states already have boards (LibraryCollapsed build.py:4373, SkillDetailRailClosed build.py:4377), which is more than most rows in this catalogue can say.
- **AC-04** — Real, on four boards plus three baselines. (1) MarketplaceFull (build.py:2313), SettingsMachine (:2317), SettingsTeams (:2318) and SettingsInbox (:2319) are ambiguous between 'window' and 'content extent' - relabel them 'content extent - the real window scrolls', or re-render one at FRAME_H with a scrollbar drawn, so nobody reads a 1340px window as intended chrome. (2) tooltip() (build.py:463, nowrap at :469) needs max-width: 320px; white-space: normal. (3) user-select: none (build.py:301, :3965) narrows to chrome. (4) The roster's second line (build.py:3054) needs overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis to match the name line above it (build.py:3053) - gap-8.md:229 records it already over
- **AC-05** — Three generator-side fixes, all small and all independent of any translation: short_ago() (build.py:1779-1784) must stop re-parsing ago()'s English output; the fixture clock (TODAY, build.py:473) must be injectable so the design's own gates do not rot as real time moves; and the rendered shell needs a lang attribute (0 occurrences today). The macOS-only command glyphs in SHORTCUTS (build.py:3403) need a Ctrl variant, but that is AC-06's platform split, not a localisation.

Reading the table: a SIMPLE · app or SIMPLE · design row is **not** a `GAPS.md` candidate in the file's sense (the app derives it, or Teddy restates a string) — it is listed so the loop's file can be extended deliberately rather than by omission. `PF-08` is the one HARD row with no `GAPS.md` line today, and under `BRIEF.md:6` it gets a line, not a flag.

## 9. Evidence

- Catalogues: `costing/catalogue.final.json` (180 rows), `costing/catalogue.settings.json` (16), `costing/corrections.settings.json` (32, applied by the reconcilers).
